Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a electrical_engineering algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 76. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 417/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 76 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electrical_engineering structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 417. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
